# EEST cases expected to fail in CI's unchecked run. The holbuild workflow
# fails if any case not listed here does not pass; a listed case that passes
# is reported in the job summary, so it can be removed from this list.
# One case name (line 1 of its result .nsv file) per line.
#
# This branch runs the Amsterdam fixture set (`fork_name` in
# tests/vfmTestAuxLib.sml), so the `fork_Osaka` entries this list carries on
# main do not run here; their `fork_Amsterdam` counterparts below cover the
# same cases, including the requests from modified or failing system contracts
# (verifereum/verifereum#177), which Amsterdam still tests. The list is the
# Amsterdam work still to be done: as an EIP lands, its cases come out.
